# ==============================================================================
#  猩红剧团 ·「酒神的大地巡演」—— scripted_gui
#
#  · SCR_Bacchanal_MapIcons : state_mapicon。面板打开时，在剧团控制的未巡演州
#                             上画一枚面具标记；点击 = 把该州选为巡演舞台。
#  · SCR_Bacchanal_GUI      : player_context 独立浮窗；剧目切换 / 启程 / 关闭。
#
#  ⚠️ 控件名必须与 interface/_ARF_Gui/SCR_Bacchanal.gui 完全一致。
#  ⚠️ state_mapicon 的 effects 默认作用域是「被点的州」，ROOT 才是玩家国；
#    target_trigger 仍按 targeted decision 写法（ROOT=玩家国，FROM=目标州）。
# ==============================================================================

scripted_gui = {

	# ---------------- 地图上的逐州巡演标记 ----------------
	SCR_Bacchanal_MapIcons = {
		context_type = state_mapicon
		window_name = "SCR_Bacchanal_MapIcons"

		# 可见性放在 target_trigger 里（与 targeted decision 同作用域：ROOT=玩家国，FROM=目标州），
		# 避免 state_mapicon 的 visible 在州作用域里误读国家旗标。
		visible = { always = yes }

		ai_enabled = { always = no }

		mapicon_targets = {
			target_array = global.states
			target_trigger = {
				is_ai = no
				FROM = {
					NOT = { has_state_flag = scr_bac_visited }
				}
				OR = {
					# 全世界模式：即使面板关着也保留州图标，点一下会重新打开面板并选中
					check_variable = { SCR_tour_all = 1 }
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = scr_bac_open
						FROM = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		effects = {
			SCR_Bac_MapIcon_click = {
				SCR_Bacchanal_SelectState = yes
			}
		}
	}


	# ---------------- 独立浮动面板 ----------------
	SCR_Bacchanal_GUI = {
		context_type = player_context
		window_name = "SCR_Bacchanal_Window"

				SCR_Bacchanal_Close = yes
			}

			SCR_Bac_Card_1_click = {
				set_variable = { SCR_tour_play = 1 }
				add_to_variable = { SCR_bac_tick = 1 }
			}
			SCR_Bac_Card_2_click = {
				set_variable = { SCR_tour_play = 2 }
				add_to_variable = { SCR_bac_tick = 1 }
			}

				set_variable = { SCR_tour_play = 3 }

				SCR_Bacchanal_Depart = yes
			}

			SCR_Bac_Pick_click = {
				SCR_Bacchanal_RandomPick = yes
			}

			SCR_Bac_AllOn_click = {
				set_variable = { SCR_tour_all = 1 }
				every_state = { clr_state_flag = scr_bac_selected }
				set_variable = { SCR_tour_state = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { SCR_bac_tick = 1 }
				force_update_map_mode = {
					limit = { always = yes }
					mapmode = scr_bacchanal_map_mode
				}
			}

			SCR_Bac_AllOff_click = {
				set_variable = { SCR_tour_all = 0 }
				every_state = { clr_state_flag = scr_bac_selected }
				set_variable = { SCR_tour_state = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { SCR_bac_tick = 1 }
				force_update_map_mode = {
					limit = { always = yes }
					mapmode = scr_bacchanal_map_mode
				}
			}

				any_state = {
					has_state_flag = scr_bac_selected
					NOT = { has_state_flag = scr_bac_visited }
				}
			}

			SCR_Bac_Pick_click_enabled = {
				OR = {

						NOT = { has_state_flag = scr_bac_visited }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { SCR_tour_all = 1 }
						any_state = {
							NOT = { has_state_flag = scr_bac_visited }
						}
					}
				}
			}
